Lately I just kind of stand there feeling flat, and I usually make up some excuse to leave.\n\nDoctor: Have you been feeling down or depressed most of the day, nearly every day?\n\nPatient: I’m not really sure how to answer that. I know I’ve been feeling off and kind of low a lot, but I haven’t been keeping track of whether it’s most of the day every day.\n\nDoctor: Would you say that most days, you feel down or depressed for most of the day?\n\nPatient: I don’t know if I’d say most of the day. It’s more like I notice it a lot, especially when I’m just trying to get through work or go home. But I haven’t really been tracking how long it lasts.\n\nDoctor: Do you find that most days, your mood is down or depressed for the majority of the day?\n\nPatient: I honestly don’t know. I know I feel low a lot of days, but I can’t really say it’s most of the day or the majority of the day. I haven’t been paying attention to it that closely.\n\nDoctor: Do you find that most days you experience a low or depressed mood, even if it doesn't last all day?\n\nPatient: Yeah… I think most days I do feel kind of low at some point. I’m not sure how long it lasts, but it does seem to come up pretty often.\n\nDoctor: Have you found that you don’t get any pleasure out of things you used to enjoy, like gallery nights, basketball, or helping kids with art?\n\nPatient: Yeah… I used to look forward to stuff like that. Gallery nights, playing basketball, getting tacos from the food trucks, helping kids with art projects. Lately I’ll go, but I just kind of stand there feeling flat. I don’t really enjoy it, and most of the time I end up making some excuse to leave.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel down or depressed for most of the day, even if it’s not the entire day?\n\nPatient: I’m not really sure if it’s most of the day. I feel down pretty often, but I don’t track how long it lasts. Some days it feels like it’s there a lot, but I can’t say for sure.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel down or depressed for most of the day on most days?\n\nPatient: I honestly don’t know if it’s most of the day on most days. I know I feel down a lot, but I’m not really keeping track of how long it lasts.\n\nDoctor: Can you say for sure if, on most days, your mood is down or depressed for most of the day, even if not the entire day?\n\nPatient: No, I can’t say for sure. I know I feel down a lot, but I haven’t really kept track enough to know if it’s most of the day on most days.\n\nDoctor: Can you think back over the last few weeks—on most days, does your mood stay low or depressed for the majority of the day, even if it’s not the whole day?\n\nPatient: I’ve been trying to think about it, but I still can’t really say for sure. My mood’s been low a lot, yeah, but I don’t know if it’s actually the majority of the day on most days.\n\nDoctor: Looking back over the past two weeks, on most days did you feel down or depressed for most of the day, even if not the entire day?\n\nPatient: I still can’t really say for sure. I know I’ve felt down a lot over the past couple weeks, but I haven’t paid attention enough to know if it was most of the day on most days.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed a lack of interest or pleasure in activities you used to enjoy, even when you make yourself do them?\n\nPatient: Yeah. Lately, even if I make myself go, I just kind of stand there feeling flat, and I usually end up making some excuse to leave.\n\nDoctor: Looking back over the past two weeks, on most days did you feel down or depressed for most of the day, even if not the entire day?\n\nPatient: I’m still not sure how to answer that exactly. I know I’ve been down a lot, but I haven’t really tracked whether it was most of the day on most days.\n\nDoctor: Looking back over the past two weeks, would you say that on most days, your mood was down or depressed for the majority of the day, even if not the entire day?\n\nPatient: I honestly don’t know if it was the majority of the day.
